## Forward reference

`rem-elementary-moves-do-not-constitute-full-cerf-theory-here` → `thm-high-dimensional-whitney-trick`: orientation-reviewed. The current source has only `## Remarks`; its single target link explicitly defers removal of excess geometric intersections. It asserts no one-parameter connectivity or classification and relies on cancellation only under the exact single-transverse-intersection premise. The target is already authored: its Statement has complementary oriented sheets with a,b≥3, opposite intersection signs, a specified nullhomotopic Whitney circle and AC_omega, and concludes an ambient isotopy removing exactly the two points. Nothing in the source claims that theorem at the earlier page. In current `research/plan-spec.json`, source page `handle-cancellation-slides-and-elementary-moves` has order 533 and target page `the-whitney-trick-and-surgery-below-the-middle-dimension` has order 559. No proof dependency was promoted. This review binds current source/target bytes in the forward row; no defect was found and no carrier was edited.

## Local correction: lifted Schubert cells

`lem-oriented-grassmannian-has-two-lifted-schubert-cells` (batch 30), F2, incorrectly asserted that stable `BO(n)` has a finite CW structure. The actual published supplier `thm-schubert-cells-give-the-stable-grassmannian-cw-structure`, Statement and Proof 4.1, distinguishes finite Grassmannian stages from their infinite union and proves the pivot bound `a_i <= i+d`, which makes each bounded-dimensional skeleton finite. For example `BO(1)=RP∞` has one cell in every nonnegative dimension, so the old finite-total-cell assertion is false. This is a confirmed nonfatal citation defect: the local Statement and its double-cover/CW conclusion are correct, and Proof 2.1 needs finiteness only of each bounded-dimensional skeleton.

Repair: F2 now names finite stages, finite skeleta, the stable weak CW topology and exact characteristic-frame source locators; Proof 3.1 specifies finite counts **in each dimension** and cites F2 for that assertion. The Statement, Definition, dependencies, manifest statement/strategy and provenance remain accurate and unchanged. Batch and merged proof contracts were regenerated/refreshed for this one item. The previous independent risk review is preserved in `risk_review_history`; the new CRITICAL review records the complete current cover, disk-lift, finite quotient and weak-topology checks, rank-zero/rank-one cases and exact AC use. There was no judge record to invalidate. No published item was edited. No Statement or Definition changed, so it causes no Statement/Definition propagation. The surface checker also includes Facts; its four direct consumers are reviewed below.

Defect owner: `frontier-41-ha-dt-29-5b-stable-grassmannian-finite-stage-citation`, closed at `5b-cross`, uniquely owned by the current-hash item verdict. The published supplier itself is sound on this issue; no new published-defect record is warranted.

The 85 edges to `def-countable-choice-principle-for-foliation-pair` were reviewed as assumption/definition uses. Each source declares AC_omega in its hypothesis or explicitly in its Facts & Assumptions; the target is exactly the N-indexed sequence-of-nonempty-sets principle, with the product equivalence certified separately. Those edge decisions do not assert a new proof audit of every foliation theorem, and they do not license arbitrary-index Choice or dependent-choice inferences.

Focused checks on the repaired item: risk-report before editing identified CRITICAL (score 11); after editing its complete risk review passed `--require-reviewed`. Reflow was unchanged, precheck checked one item and passed, and scoped strict proof-contract returned no errors or warnings. The carrier command returned `395b876fac389ac81ed45960b4647731e3b1930979f9f678ee57bd3e74eda9d6` after contract refresh. No engine gate battery or judge cycle was run.

## Live impact window after the local repair

`post-5a → current --direct-boundary` recomputes one changed surface and four consumers because the surface fingerprint includes Facts. All four consumers remain still-licensed on current content:

- `def-thom-prespectrum-of-the-universal-real-and-oriented-bundles`: The declared dependency and the oriented Thom CW construction consume the two lifted base cells, weak topology and finite boundary support. The Definition separately constructs the dimension-shifted Thom cells, finite-stage compact quotients and compact-test union topology. It never assumes finitely many cells in all of stable BSO(n). The corrected finite-stage/per-dimension qualification preserves every consumed clause; rank zero remains the separate S0 level.
- `prop-products-of-complex-projective-spaces-span-rational-oriented-bordism`: Facts F1 uses lifted d-cells and the Thom (d+r)-cell description to establish absence of non-basepoint cells below dimension r; Proof 1.1 uses only that connectivity input. Its finite-range rational Hurewicz comparison never requires globally finite BO(r) or BSO(r). The current Statement of the lifted-cover supplier gives exactly the lifted CW and finite-boundary-support clauses, preserved after F2 correction.
- `thm-bo-bso-cohomology-away-from-two`: Facts F1 and Proof 1.1 consume the lifted weak CW structure, two cells above each ordinary Schubert cell and the oriented universal models. The even/odd-rank Gysin induction uses these CW bases, not a finite-total-cell claim. The actual ring hypotheses 2 invertible and nonzero R remain separate. Corrected finite-stage/per-dimension finiteness preserves this use.
- `thm-integral-finite-generation-of-mo-and-mso-homology`: Facts F4 and Proof 8.1 consume the two lifts of each base Schubert cell. For a fixed dimension d, the pivot bound a_i<=i+d gives finitely many ordinary cells, hence finitely many lifted cells. The proof therefore has finite free cellular chains in each degree and finite generation, with rank zero handled separately. It never requires finitely many cells in the whole stable base.

These uses are recorded with exact supplier and consumer raw hashes in `research/frontier-41-ha-dt-29-impact-5b.json`. No consumer Statement or Definition changed, so no further propagation hop opens.
